record.userId !== data.userId ||\n456\t      !TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(record.channelId) ||\n457\t      !TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(record.messageId)\n458\t    ) {\n459\t      this.logger.warn(\n460\t null;\n469\t    }\n470\t\n471\t    return this.withTenantQueryRunner(data.tenantId, async (queryRunner) => {\n472\t      const member = await queryRunner.manager.findOne(ChannelMember, {\n473\t        where: {\n474\t          channelId: record.channelId,\n475\t          userId: data.userId,\n476\t          leftAt: IsNull(),\n477\t        },\n478\t      });\n479\t      if (!member) {\n480\t        return null;\n481\t      }\n482\t\n483\t      const messageRows: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n484\t        `SELECT EXISTS(\n485\t           SELECT 1\n486\t           FROM messages\n487\t           WHERE id = $1\n488\t             AND \"channelId\" = $2\n489\t             AND \"createdAt\" = $3::timestamptz\n490\t             AND \"isDeleted\" = false\n491\t        ) AS exists`,\n492\t        [record.messageId, record.channelId, record.messageCreatedAt],\n493\t      );\n494\t      if (!booleanColumn(messageRows, 'exists')) {\n495\t        return null;\n496\t      }\n497\t\n498\t      return {\n499\t        channelId: record.channelId,\n500\t        messageId: record.messageId,\n501\t        messageCreatedAt: record.messageCreatedAt,\n502\t      };\n503\t    });\n504\t  }\n505\t\n506\t  /**\n507\t   * Handles user deletion: anonymises messages, removes channel memberships,\n508\t   * and cleans up reactions/receipts in a single transaction.\n509\t   * Pins tenant schema before executing any queries.\n510\t   */\n511\t  @EventPattern('events.*.UserDeleted')\n512\t  async handleUserDeleted(@Payload() data: UserDeletedPayload): Promise<void> {\n513\t    const deletedUserId = data.deletedUserId;\n514\t    if (!TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(data.tenantId) || !TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(deletedUserId)) {\n515\t      this.logger.error(\n516\t        'Rejected UserDeleted event missing canonical deletedUserId or valid tenantId',\n517\t      );\n518\t      return;\n519\t    }\n520\t\n521\t    this.logger.log(\n522\t      `Processing UserDeleted for deletedUserId ${deletedUserId} in tenant ${data.tenantId}`,\n523\t    );\n524\t\n525\t    try {\n526\t      // MSG-CRITICAL-058: attachment MinIO object keys collected inside the tx for\n527\t      // the erased (non-held) messages; purged AFTER commit so the DB row is already\n528\t      // gone before we drop the binary (a failed purge leaves an orphan, not a live\n529\t      // PII reference). Held channels are excluded — legal hold preserves their media.\n530\t      const objectKeysToPurge = await this.withTenantQueryRunner(\n531\t        data.tenantId,\n532\t        async (queryRunner) => {\n533\t          // SECURITY: Verify user has actual presence in claimed tenant before destructive cascade\n534\t          const userMessages: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n535\t            `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM messages WHERE \"senderId\" = $1 LIMIT 1) AS has_messages`,\n536\t            [deletedUserId],\n537\t          );\n538\t          const userMemberships: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n539\t            `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM channel_members WHERE \"userId\" = $1 LIMIT 1) AS has_memberships`,\n540\t            [deletedUserId],\n541\t          );\n542\t\n543\t          if (\n544\t            !booleanColumn(userMessages, 'has_messages') &&\n545\t            !booleanColumn(userMemberships, 'has_memberships')\n546\t          ) {\n547\t            this.logger.log(\n548\t              `UserDeleted: deletedUserId=${deletedUserId} has no messaging footprint in tenant ${data.tenantId}, skipping cascade`,\n549\t            );\n550\t            return [];\n551\t          }\n552\t\n553\t          // Collect ALL message IDs for this user BEFORE any anonymization.\n554\t          // WHY: after we set senderId=ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, we can no longer identify\n555\t          // which messages belonged to this specific user — ANONYMOUS_USER_ID is shared\n556\t          // across all deleted users. We need the IDs up front to clean AI-derived tables.\n557\t          const userMsgRowsResult: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n558\t            `SELECT id, \"channelId\" FROM messages WHERE \"senderId\" = $1`,\n559\t            [deletedUserId],\n560\t          );\n561\t          const userMsgRows = Array.isArray(userMsgRowsResult)\n562\t            ? userMsgRowsResult.filter(isMessageChannelRow)\n563\t            : [];\n564\t          const userMessageIds = userMsgRows.map((r) => r.id);\n565\t\n566\t          // Determine which channels the user has messages in\n567\t          const channelRows = userMsgRows.reduce<Array<{ channelId: string }>>((acc, r) => {\n568\t            if (!acc.some((x) => x.channelId === r.channelId)) {\n569\t              acc.push({ channelId: r.channelId });\n570\t            }\n571\t            return acc;\n572\t          }, []);\n573\t\n574\t          // For each channel, check legal hold status and anonymize accordingly.\n575\t          // BEFORE: all messages wiped unconditionally — messages in litigation-held\n576\t          // channels had content destroyed, creating spoliation liability.\n577\t          // WHY: Legal hold requires content preservation. We still anonymize the senderId\n578\t          // (to protect the user's identity under GDPR) but preserve content in held channels.\n579\t          const heldChannelIds = new Set<string>();\n580\t          for (const { channelId } of channelRows) {\n581\t            const isHeld = await this.legalHoldService.isUnderLegalHold(data.tenantId, channelId);\n582\t            if (isHeld) {\n583\t              heldChannelIds.add(channelId);\n584\t              // Held channel: anonymize sender identity only, preserve content\n585\t              await queryRunner.query(\n586\t                `UPDATE messages SET \"senderId\" = $1 WHERE \"senderId\" = $2 AND \"channelId\" = $3`,\n587\t                [ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, deletedUserId, channelId],\n588\t              );\n589\t            }\n590\t          }\n591\t\n592\t          // MSG-CRITICAL-058: for messages in NON-HELD channels (whose content we are\n593\t          // about to wipe), delete the attachment rows and collect their MinIO object\n594\t          // keys so the binaries are purged after commit. Held-channel attachments are\n595\t          // preserved — legal hold outranks erasure. Mirrors GdprService.anonymizeMyData.\n596\t          const erasableMessageIds = userMsgRows\n597\t            .filter((r) => !heldChannelIds.has(r.channelId))\n598\t            .map((r) => r.id);\n599\t          const objectKeys: string[] = [];\n600\t          if (erasableMessageIds.length > 0) {\n601\t            const attachmentRows: Array<{ storageKey: string; thumbnailKey: string | null }> =\n602\t              await queryRunner.query(\n603\t                `SELECT \"storageKey\", \"thumbnailKey\" FROM message_attachments\n604\t               WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n605\t                [erasableMessageIds],\n606\t              );\n607\t            for (const row of attachmentRows) {\n608\t              if (row.storageKey) objectKeys.push(row.storageKey);\n609\t              if (row.thumbnailKey) objectKeys.push(row.thumbnailKey);\n610\t            }\n611\t            await queryRunner.query(\n612\t              `DELETE FROM message_attachments WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n613\t              [erasableMessageIds],\n614\t            );\n615\t          }\n616\t\n617\t          // Non-held channels: anonymize sender + wipe content + clear embedding.\n618\t          // BEFORE: embedding column was NOT cleared — vector index retained the user's\n619\t          // original message content even after anonymization, enabling re-identification\n620\t          // via semantic similarity search. GdprService.anonymizeMyData() correctly\n621\t          // sets embedding=NULL; this handler now aligns with that behavior.\n622\t          if (heldChannelIds.size < channelRows.length) {\n623\t            const heldIds = Array.from(heldChannelIds);\n624\t            const whereClause =\n625\t              heldIds.length > 0\n626\t                ? `\"senderId\" = $2 AND \"channelId\" != ALL($3::uuid[])`\n627\t                : `\"senderId\" = $2`;\n628\t            const params =\n629\t              heldIds.length > 0\n630\t                ? [ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, deletedUserId, heldIds]\n631\t                : [ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, deletedUserId];\n632\t\n633\t            await queryRunner.query(\n634\t              `UPDATE messages\n635\t             SET \"senderId\" = $1,\n636\t                 content = '[message deleted by user]',\n637\t                 embedding = NULL\n638\t             WHERE ${whereClause}`,\n639\t              params,\n640\t            );\n641\t          }\n642\t\n643\t          // Clean AI-derived PII using the message IDs collected before anonymization.\n644\t          // BEFORE: message_entity_references and message_analysis rows were never cleaned.\n645\t          // Also: there was a bug where IDs were collected AFTER anonymization by querying\n646\t          // senderId=ANONYMOUS_USER_ID — which would return ALL anonymized users' messages.\n647\t          // Using pre-collected userMessageIds is the correct approach.\n648\t          if (userMessageIds.length > 0) {\n649\t            await queryRunner.query(\n650\t              `DELETE FROM message_entity_references WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n651\t              [userMessageIds],\n652\t            );\n653\t            await queryRunner.query(\n654\t              `DELETE FROM message_analysis WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n655\t              [userMessageIds],\n656\t            );\n657\t          }\n658\t\n659\t          // Remove reactions\n660\t          await queryRunner.query(`DELETE FROM message_reactions WHERE \"userId\" = $1`, [\n661\t            deletedUserId,\n662\t          ]);\n663\t\n664\t          // Remove receipts\n665\t          await queryRunner.query(`DELETE FROM message_receipts WHERE \"userId\" = $1`, [\n666\t            deletedUserId,\n667\t          ]);\n668\t\n669\t          // Mark channel memberships as left\n670\t          await queryRunner.query(\n671\t            `UPDATE channel_members SET \"leftAt\" = NOW() WHERE \"userId\" = $1 AND \"leftAt\" IS NULL`,\n672\t            [deletedUserId],\n673\t          );\n674\t\n675\t          return objectKeys;\n676\t        },\n677\t      );\n678\t\n679\t      // MSG-CRITICAL-058: the attachment ROWS are committed-deleted; purge the MinIO"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c5bb5fdf-5a26-4183-87ff-c9f8f8a20476","uuid":"6b132fdd-bb90-47af-a072-7f8cc107e17a","timestamp":"2026-10-08T02:07:02.394Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-eb0ad351ae6b/apps/messaging-service/src/event-handlers/messaging-nats.handler.ts","content":" (Number.isNaN(messageCreatedAt.getTime())) {\n      this.logger.warn('Rejected notificationRef with invalid messageCreatedAt');\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return this.withTenantQueryRunner(data.tenantId, async (queryRunner) => {\n      const member = await queryRunner.manager.findOne(ChannelMember, {\n        where: {\n          channelId: record.channelId,\n          userId: data.userId,\n          leftAt: IsNull(),\n        },\n      });\n      if (!member) {\n        return null;\n      }\n\n      const messageRows: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n        `SELECT EXISTS(\n           SELECT 1\n           FROM messages\n           WHERE id = $1\n             AND \"channelId\" = $2\n             AND \"createdAt\" = $3::timestamptz\n             AND \"isDeleted\" = false\n        ) AS exists`,\n        [record.messageId, record.channelId, record.messageCreatedAt],\n      );\n      if (!booleanColumn(messageRows, 'exists')) {\n        return null;\n      }\n\n      return {\n        channelId: record.channelId,\n        messageId: record.messageId,\n        messageCreatedAt: record.messageCreatedAt,\n      };\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Handles user deletion: anonymises messages, removes channel memberships,\n   * and cleans up reactions/receipts in a single transaction.\n   * Pins tenant schema before executing any queries.\n   */\n  @EventPattern('events.*.UserDeleted')\n  async handleUserDeleted(@Payload() data: UserDeletedPayload): Promise<void> {\n    const deletedUserId = data.deletedUserId;\n    if (!TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(data.tenantId) || !TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(deletedUserId)) {\n      this.logger.error(\n        'Rejected UserDeleted event missing canonical deletedUserId or valid tenantId',\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n\n    this.logger.log(\n      `Processing UserDeleted for deletedUserId ${deletedUserId} in tenant ${data.tenantId}`,\n    );\n\n    try {\n      // MSG-CRITICAL-058: attachment MinIO object keys collected inside the tx for\n      // the erased (non-held) messages; purged AFTER commit so the DB row is already\n      // gone before we drop the binary (a failed purge leaves an orphan, not a live\n      // PII reference). Held channels are excluded — legal hold preserves their media.\n      const objectKeysToPurge = await this.withTenantQueryRunner(\n        data.tenantId,\n        async (queryRunner) => {\n          // SECURITY: Verify user has actual presence in claimed tenant before destructive cascade\n          const userMessages: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n            `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM messages WHERE \"senderId\" = $1 LIMIT 1) AS has_messages`,\n            [deletedUserId],\n          );\n          const userMemberships: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n            `SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM channel_members WHERE \"userId\" = $1 LIMIT 1) AS has_memberships`,\n            [deletedUserId],\n          );\n\n          if (\n            !booleanColumn(userMessages, 'has_messages') &&\n            !booleanColumn(userMemberships, 'has_memberships')\n          ) {\n            this.logger.log(\n              `UserDeleted: deletedUserId=${deletedUserId} has no messaging footprint in tenant ${data.tenantId}, skipping cascade`,\n            );\n            return [];\n          }\n\n          // Collect ALL message IDs for this user BEFORE any anonymization.\n          // WHY: after we set senderId=ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, we can no longer identify\n          // which messages belonged to this specific user — ANONYMOUS_USER_ID is shared\n          // across all deleted users. We need the IDs up front to clean AI-derived tables.\n          const userMsgRowsResult: unknown = await queryRunner.query(\n            `SELECT id, \"channelId\" FROM messages WHERE \"senderId\" = $1`,\n            [deletedUserId],\n          );\n          const userMsgRows = Array.isArray(userMsgRowsResult)\n            ? userMsgRowsResult.filter(isMessageChannelRow)\n            : [];\n          const userMessageIds = userMsgRows.map((r) => r.id);\n\n          // Determine which channels the user has messages in\n          const channelRows = userMsgRows.reduce<Array<{ channelId: string }>>((acc, r) => {\n            if (!acc.some((x) => x.channelId === r.channelId)) {\n              acc.push({ channelId: r.channelId });\n            }\n            return acc;\n          }, []);\n\n          // For each channel, check legal hold status and anonymize accordingly.\n          // BEFORE: all messages wiped unconditionally — messages in litigation-held\n          // channels had content destroyed, creating spoliation liability.\n          // WHY: Legal hold requires content preservation. We still anonymize the senderId\n          // (to protect the user's identity under GDPR) but preserve content in held channels.\n          const heldChannelIds = new Set<string>();\n          for (const { channelId } of channelRows) {\n            const isHeld = await this.legalHoldService.isUnderLegalHold(data.tenantId, channelId);\n            if (isHeld) {\n              heldChannelIds.add(channelId);\n              // Held channel: anonymize sender identity only, preserve content\n              await queryRunner.query(\n                `UPDATE messages SET \"senderId\" = $1 WHERE \"senderId\" = $2 AND \"channelId\" = $3`,\n                [ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, deletedUserId, channelId],\n              );\n            }\n          }\n\n          // MSG-CRITICAL-058: for messages in NON-HELD channels (whose content we are\n          // about to wipe), delete the attachment rows and collect their MinIO object\n          // keys so the binaries are purged after commit. Held-channel attachments are\n          // preserved — legal hold outranks erasure. Mirrors GdprService.anonymizeMyData.\n          const erasableMessageIds = userMsgRows\n            .filter((r) => !heldChannelIds.has(r.channelId))\n            .map((r) => r.id);\n          const objectKeys: string[] = [];\n          if (erasableMessageIds.length > 0) {\n            const attachmentRows: Array<{ storageKey: string; thumbnailKey: string | null }> =\n              await queryRunner.query(\n                `SELECT \"storageKey\", \"thumbnailKey\" FROM message_attachments\n               WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n                [erasableMessageIds],\n              );\n            for (const row of attachmentRows) {\n              if (row.storageKey) objectKeys.push(row.storageKey);\n              if (row.thumbnailKey) objectKeys.push(row.thumbnailKey);\n            }\n            await queryRunner.query(\n              `DELETE FROM message_attachments WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n              [erasableMessageIds],\n            );\n          }\n\n          // Non-held channels: anonymize sender + wipe content + clear embedding.\n          // BEFORE: embedding column was NOT cleared — vector index retained the user's\n          // original message content even after anonymization, enabling re-identification\n          // via semantic similarity search. GdprService.anonymizeMyData() correctly\n          // sets embedding=NULL; this handler now aligns with that behavior.\n          if (heldChannelIds.size < channelRows.length) {\n            const heldIds = Array.from(heldChannelIds);\n            const whereClause =\n              heldIds.length > 0\n                ? `\"senderId\" = $2 AND \"channelId\" != ALL($3::uuid[])`\n                : `\"senderId\" = $2`;\n            const params =\n              heldIds.length > 0\n                ? [ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, deletedUserId, heldIds]\n                : [ANONYMOUS_USER_ID, deletedUserId];\n\n            await queryRunner.query(\n              `UPDATE messages\n             SET \"senderId\" = $1,\n                 content = '[message deleted by user]',\n                 embedding = NULL\n             WHERE ${whereClause}`,\n              params,\n            );\n          }\n\n          // Clean AI-derived PII using the message IDs collected before anonymization.\n          // BEFORE: message_entity_references and message_analysis rows were never cleaned.\n          // Also: there was a bug where IDs were collected AFTER anonymization by querying\n          // senderId=ANONYMOUS_USER_ID — which would return ALL anonymized users' messages.\n          // Using pre-collected userMessageIds is the correct approach.\n          if (userMessageIds.length > 0) {\n            await queryRunner.query(\n              `DELETE FROM message_entity_references WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n              [userMessageIds],\n            );\n            await queryRunner.query(\n              `DELETE FROM message_analysis WHERE \"messageId\" = ANY($1::uuid[])`,\n              [userMessageIds],\n            );\n          }\n\n          // Remove reactions\n          await queryRunner.query(`DELETE FROM message_reactions WHERE \"userId\" = $1`, [\n            deletedUserId,\n          ]);\n\n          // Remove receipts\n          await queryRunner.query(`DELETE FROM message_receipts WHERE \"userId\" = $1`, [\n            deletedUserId,\n          ]);\n\n          // Mark channel memberships as left\n          await queryRunner.query(\n            `UPDATE channel_members SET \"leftAt\" = NOW() WHERE \"userId\" = $1 AND \"leftAt\" IS NULL`,\n            [deletedUserId],\n          );\n\n          return objectKeys;\n        },\n      );\n\n      // MSG-CRITICAL-058: the attachment ROWS are committed-deleted; purge the MinIO","numLines":240,"startLine":440,"totalLines":764}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Nf3iQreF6Lf56XBc9aBMS3","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\n2\timport { DataSource, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n3\t\n4\timport { withTenantContext } from '../context/with-tenant-context';\n5\timport { getRequestContext } from '../logging/request-context';\n6\t\n7\timport { RLS_BYPASS_GUC, RLS_TENANT_GUC } from './rls/apply-tenant-rls.helper';\n8\timport { validateTenantSchemaName } from './schema-manager.service';\n9\timport { TenantContextError } from './tenant-context-error';\n10\timport { getTenantSchemaName, isValidUUID } from './tenant-schema.utils';\n11\t\n12\tconst SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/;\n13\t\n14\t/**\n15\t * Tenant boundary observability (Farm Data SSOT plan §3-F / §8.7).\n16\t *\n17\t * Every boundary execution emits one structured trace so a read's tenant\n18\t * routing is observable end to end. `resultState` is the FarmDataReadTrace\n19\t * taxonomy: a verified `SUCCESS`, or the exact failure mode that previously\n20\t * produced a silent empty result. SUCCESS traces are debug-level (off in prod\n21\t * by default); a tenant-context mismatch is warn-level. Emitting the trace must\n22\t * NEVER break a read — any error inside the tracer is swallowed.\n23\t */\n24\texport type TenantBoundaryResultState = 'SUCCESS' | 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH' | 'RLS_MISMATCH' | 'ERROR';\n25\t\n26\tconst boundaryLogger = new Logger('TenantBoundary');\n27\t\n28\t/** Best-effort row count for the common array / paginated-`data` result shapes. */\n29\tfunction rowCountOf(result: unknown): number | undefined {\n30\t  if (Array.isArray(result)) return result.length;\n31\t  if (result && typeof result === 'object' && Array.isArray((result as { data?: unknown }).data)) {\n32\t    return (result as { data: unknown[] }).data.length;\n33\t  }\n34\t  return undefined;\n35\t}\n36\t\n37\tinterface TenantBoundaryTraceInput {\n38\t  readonly operation: 'tenant-read' | 'tenant-transaction' | 'source-read';\n39\t  readonly sourceSchema: string;\n40\t  readonly tenantId?: string;\n41\t  readonly resultState: TenantBoundaryResultState;\n42\t  readonly startedAt: number;\n43\t  readonly result?: unknown;\n44\t  readonly error?: unknown;\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\tfunction traceTenantBoundary(input: TenantBoundaryTraceInput): void {\n48\t  try {\n49\t    const ctx = getRequestContext();\n50\t    const trace = {\n51\t      event: 'TenantBoundaryTrace',\n52\t      operation: input.operation,\n53\t      resultState: input.resultState,\n54\t      correlationId: ctx.correlationId,\n55\t      traceId: ctx.traceId,\n56\t      durationMs: Date.now() - input.startedAt,\n57\t      rowCount: rowCountOf(input.result),\n58\t    };\n59\t    if (input.resultState === 'SUCCESS') {\n60\t      // Success is one trace per read (high volume). It is OPT-IN — enable with\n61\t      // FARM_DATA_READ_TRACE=on for deep per-read diagnostics. A tenant-context\n62\t      // mismatch ALWAYS emits (warn) regardless of the flag.\n63\t      if (process.env['FARM_DATA_READ_TRACE'] === 'on') {\n64\t        boundaryLogger.debug(JSON.stringify(trace));\n65\t      }\n66\t    } else {\n67\t      boundaryLogger.warn(JSON.stringify(trace));\n68\t    }\n69\t  } catch {\n70\t    // Observability must never break a read.\n71\t  }\n72\t}\n73\t\n74\tfunction resultStateForError(error: unknown): TenantBoundaryResultState {\n75\t  if (error instanceof TenantContextError) {\n76\t    return error.state === 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH'\n77\t      ? 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH'\n78\t      : error.state === 'RLS_MISMATCH'\n79\t        ? 'RLS_MISMATCH'\n80\t        : 'ERROR';\n81\t  }\n82\t  return 'ERROR';\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\t/**\n86\t * Pin a QueryRunner transaction to one tenant schema.\n87\t *\n88\t * This is the transaction-level counterpart to TenantConnectionBootstrap's\n89\t * pool checkout patch. Checkout routing is necessary but not sufficient for\n90\t * long-lived transaction code: every transactional write should explicitly\n91\t * assert its own `search_path` after `START TRANSACTION`, so a stale pooled\n92\t * session or missing AsyncLocalStorage frame cannot write source-schema data.\n93\t */\n94\texport async function pinTenantTransactionSearchPath(\n95\t  queryRunner: QueryRunner,\n96\t  sourceSchema: string,\n97\t  tenantId: string,\n98\t): Promise<void> {\n99\t  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n100\t    throw new Error(`pinTenantTransactionSearchPath: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n101\t  }\n102\t  if (!isValidUUID(tenantId)) {\n103\t    throw new Error(`pinTenantTransactionSearchPath: invalid tenantId \"${tenantId}\"`);\n104\t  }\n105\t\n106\t  const tenantSchema = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n107\t  await pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantSchema);\n108\t}\n109\t\n110\texport async function pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath(\n111\t  queryRunner: QueryRunner,\n112\t  sourceSchema: string,\n113\t  tenantSchema: string,\n114\t): Promise<void> {\n115\t  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n116\t    throw new Error(\n117\t      `pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`,\n118\t    );\n119\t  }\n120\t  validateTenantSchemaName(tenantSchema);\n121\t\n122\t  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', $1, true)`, [\n123\t    `\"${tenantSchema}\", \"${sourceSchema}\", public`,\n124\t  ]);\n125\t}\n126\t\n127\t/**\n128\t * Set the `app.current_tenant` RLS GUC transaction-locally and ASSERT that the\n129\t * live connection actually resolves to the expected tenant schema + tenant id\n130\t * BEFORE any domain query runs.\n131\t *\n132\t * This is the load-bearing fail-closed guarantee (Farm Data SSOT plan §5-1).\n133\t * `RlsConnectionBootstrap` sets the GUC on pool checkout, but nothing today\n134\t * verifies it actually took effect on the connection a handler ends up using —\n135\t * so an unset GUC (RLS denies all rows) or a missing tenant schema (search_path\n136\t * silently falls back to the source schema) both produce an empty result that\n137\t * is indistinguishable from a legitimately-empty table. By owning the GUC\n138\t * transaction-locally and reading back `current_schema()` + the GUC, the\n139\t * boundary turns those two silent failure modes into a hard `TenantContextError`.\n140\t *\n141\t * `current_schema()` returns the first EXISTING schema in `search_path`, so a\n142\t * missing/un-provisioned tenant schema (which falls through to the source\n143\t * schema) is caught as a `SCHEMA_MISMATCH`.\n144\t */\n145\t/**\n146\t * The single capability the boundary assertion needs from a connection. A full\n147\t * TypeORM `QueryRunner` satisfies this, but narrowing to it keeps the assertion\n148\t * unit-testable with a minimal mock and no unsafe double cast.\n149\t */\n150\texport interface TenantContextQueryExecutor {\n151\t  query(sql: string, parameters?: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>;\n152\t}\n153\t\n154\t/**\n155\t * Bind the RLS controls transaction-locally and verify they took.\n156\t *\n157\t * ORPHAN-CRITICAL-573 — extracted from `assertTenantTransactionContext`\n158\t * because auth-service needs exactly this and NOT the schema half of it: its\n159\t * tenant lifecycle writes land in the SOURCE schema (`auth.*`), and during\n160\t * provisioning the tenant schema does not exist yet. Calling the full\n161\t * assertion there would trade one failure for another.\n162\t *\n163\t * What it guarantees: `app.current_tenant` is this tenant, `app.bypass_rls`\n164\t * is off, both transaction-locally so a pooled connection cannot carry either\n165\t * into the next caller — and both READ BACK, so a GUC that silently failed to\n166\t * apply becomes a named `TenantContextError` instead of an RLS refusal whose\n167\t * message names a table and not a cause. That distinction is the whole reason\n168\t * tenant onboarding stayed broken for months.\n169\t */\n170\texport async function bindTenantRlsContext(\n171\t  queryRunner: TenantContextQueryExecutor,\n172\t  tenantId: string,\n173\t  sourceSchema: string,\n174\t): Promise<void> {\n175\t  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n176\t    throw new Error(`bindTenantRlsContext: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n177\t  }\n178\t  if (!isValidUUID(tenantId)) {\n179\t    throw new Error(`bindTenantRlsContext: invalid tenantId \"${tenantId}\"`);\n180\t  }\n181\t\n182\t  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, $2, true)`, [RLS_TENANT_GUC, tenantId]);\n183\t  // A pooled connection may previously have served an audited system path;\n184\t  // a stale `on` would expose every row the policy is supposed to hide.\n185\t  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, 'off', true)`, [RLS_BYPASS_GUC]);\n186\t\n187\t  const rows = await queryRunner.query(\n188\t    `SELECT current_setting($1, true) AS tenant,\n189\t            current_setting($2, true) AS bypass`,\n190\t    [RLS_TENANT_GUC, RLS_BYPASS_GUC],\n191\t  );\n192\t  const row = (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows) as\n193\t    | { tenant?: string | null; bypass?: string | null }\n194\t    | undefined\n195\t    | null;\n196\t  if (!row) {\n197\t    return;\n198\t  }\n199\t\n200\t  const resolvedTenant: string | null = row.tenant ?? null;\n201\t  const resolvedBypass: string | null = row.bypass ?? null;\n202\t  if (resolvedTenant !== tenantId || (resolvedBypass ?? 'off') !== 'off') {\n203\t    throw new TenantContextError({\n204\t      state: 'RLS_MISMATCH',\n205\t      expectedSchema: getTenantSchemaName(tenantId),\n206\t      // No schema claim is being made here: this binding is used by writers\n207\t      // whose tables live in the source schema, so `resolvedSchema` stays\n208\t      // null rather than asserting something the caller never asked for.\n209\t      resolvedSchema: null,\n210\t      sourceSchema,\n211\t    });\n212\t  }\n213\t}\n214\t\n215\texport async function assertTenantTransactionContext(\n216\t  queryRunner: TenantContextQueryExecutor,\n217\t  sourceSchema: string,\n218\t  tenantId: string,\n219\t): Promise<void> {\n220\t  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n221\t    throw new Error(`assertTenantTransactionContext: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n222\t  }\n223\t  if (!isValidUUID(tenantId)) {\n224\t    throw new Error(`assertTenantTransactionContext: invalid tenantId \"${tenantId}\"`);\n225\t  }\n226\t\n227\t  const expectedSchema = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n228\t\n229\t  // The boundary OWNS the RLS GUC transaction-locally so it cannot be left\n230\t  // unset by a missing checkout patch or inherited stale from a pooled session.\n231\t  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, $2, true)`, [RLS_TENANT_GUC, tenantId]);\n232\t  // A pooled connection may previously have served an audited system path.\n233\t  // Tenant transactions own both RLS controls transaction-locally: setting\n234\t  // the tenant UUID without forcing bypass off would leave a stale `on`\n235\t  // capable of exposing every row in the otherwise-correct tenant schema.\n236\t  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, 'off', true)`, [RLS_BYPASS_GUC]);\n237\t\n238\t  // Read back the schema + GUC the connection ACTUALLY resolves to.\n239\t  const rows = await queryRunner.query(\n240\t    `SELECT current_schema() AS schema,\n241\t            current_setting($1, true) AS tenant,\n242\t            current_setting($2, true) AS bypass`,\n243\t    [RLS_TENANT_GUC, RLS_BYPASS_GUC],\n244\t  );\n245\t  const row = (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows) as\n246\t    | { schema?: string | null; tenant?: string | null; bypass?: string | null }\n247\t    | undefined\n248\t    | null;\n249\t\n250\t  // A live Postgres connection ALWAYS returns exactly one row for\n251\t  // `SELECT current_schema()`. The only way `row` is absent is a unit-test\n252\t  // mock with no backing connection — there is nothing live to assert against,\n253\t  // so skip. Every real execution returns a row and is therefore verified.\n254\t  if (!row) {\n255\t    return;\n256\t  }\n257\t\n258\t  const resolvedSchema: string | null = row.schema ?? null;\n259\t  const resolvedTenant: string | null = row.tenant ?? null;\n260\t  const resolvedBypass: string | null = row.bypass ?? null;\n261\t\n262\t  if (resolvedSchema !== expectedSchema) {\n263\t    throw new TenantContextError({\n264\t      state: 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH',\n265\t      expectedSchema,\n266\t      resolvedSchema,\n267\t      sourceSchema,\n268\t    });\n269\t  }\n270\t  if (\n271\t 'RLS_MISMATCH',\n277\t      expectedSchema,\n278\t      resolvedSchema,\n279\t      sourceSchema,\n280\t    });\n281\t  }\n282\t}\n283\t\n284\t/**\n285\t * Execute a TypeORM QueryRunner transaction inside a fail-closed tenant\n286\t * context and with transaction-local search_path pinning.\n287\t */\n288\texport async function runInTenantTransaction<T>(\n289\t  dataSource: DataSource,\n290\t  sourceSchema: string,\n291\t  tenantId: string,\n292\t  fn: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n293\t): Promise<T> {\n294\t  return withTenantContext(tenantId, async () => {\n295\t    const startedAt = Date.now();\n296\t    const queryRunner = dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n297\t    await queryRunner.connect();\n298\t    await queryRunner.startTransaction();\n299\t\n300\t    try {\n301\t      await pinTenantTransactionSearchPath(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n302\t      await assertTenantTransactionContext(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n303\t      const result = await fn(queryRunner);\n304\t      await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n305\t      traceTenantBoundary({\n306\t        operation: 'tenant-transaction',\n307\t        sourceSchema,\n308\t        tenantId,\n309\t        resultState: 'SUCCESS',\n310\t        startedAt,\n311\t        result,\n312\t      });\n313\t      return result;\n314\t    } catch (error) {\n315\t      await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n316\t      traceTenantBoundary({\n317\t        operation: 'tenant-transaction',\n318\t        sourceSchema,\n319\t        tenantId,\n320\t        resultState: resultStateForError(error),\n321\t        startedAt,\n322\t        error,\n323\t      });\n324\t      throw error;\n325\t    } finally {\n326\t      await queryRunner.release();\n327\t    }\n328\t  });\n329\t}\n330\t\n331\t/**\n332\t * Execute tenant-scoped reads in an explicit read-only transaction.\n333\t *\n334\t * Request middleware pins pooled sessions, but query handlers also need an\n335\t * explicit boundary so non-HTTP execution paths cannot accidentally read the\n336\t * source schema. A read-only transaction gives `set_config(..., true)` a stable\n337\t * transaction scope and makes accidental writes structurally fail.\n338\t */\n339\texport async function runInTenantRead<T>(\n340\t  dataSource: DataSource,\n341\t  sourceSchema: string,\n342\t  tenantId: string,\n343\t  fn: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n344\t): Promise<T> {\n345\t  return withTenantContext(tenantId, async () => {\n346\t    const startedAt = Date.now();\n347\t    const queryRunner = dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n348\t    await queryRunner.connect();\n349\t    await queryRunner.startTransaction('READ COMMITTED');\n350\t\n351\t    try {\n352\t      await queryRunner.query('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY');\n353\t      await pinTenantTransactionSearchPath(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n354\t      await assertTenantTransactionContext(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n355\t      const result = await fn(queryRunner);\n356\t      await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n357\t      traceTenantBoundary({\n358\t        operation: 'tenant-read',\n359\t        sourceSchema,\n360\t        tenantId,\n361\t        resultState: 'SUCCESS',\n362\t        startedAt,\n363\t        result,\n364\t      });\n365\t      return result;\n366\t    } catch (error) {\n367\t      await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n368\t      traceTenantBoundary({\n369\t        operation: 'tenant-read',\n370\t        sourceSchema,\n371\t        tenantId,\n372\t        resultState: resultStateForError(error),\n373\t        startedAt,\n374\t        error,\n375\t      });\n376\t      throw error;\n377\t    } finally {\n378\t      await queryRunner.release();\n379\t    }\n380\t  });\n381\t}\n382\t\n383\t/**\n384\t * Assert the connection actually resolved to the expected SOURCE schema before a\n385\t * source read runs. A stray tenant schema left in `search_path` (e.g. an\n386\t * inherited pooled session) would otherwise make a \"source\" read silently\n387\t * resolve a tenant's shadow copy.\n388\t */\n389\texport async function assertSourceReadContext(\n390\t  queryRunner: TenantContextQueryExecutor,\n391\t  sourceSchema: string,\n392\t): Promise<void> {\n393\t  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n394\t    throw new Error(`assertSourceReadContext: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n395\t  }\n396\t\n397\t  const rows = await queryRunner.query(`SELECT current_schema() AS schema`);\n398\t  const row = (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows) as\n399\t    | { schema?: string | null }\n400\t    | undefined\n401\t    | null;\n402\t\n403\t  // Absent row ⇒ unit-test mock with no backing connection; nothing to assert.\n404\t  if (!row) {\n405\t    return;\n406\t  }\n407\t\n408\t  const resolvedSchema: string | null = row.schema ?? null;\n409\t  if (resolvedSchema !== sourceSchema) {\n410\t    throw new TenantContextError({\n411\t      state: 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH',\n412\t      expectedSchema: sourceSchema,\n413\t      resolvedSchema,\n414\t      sourceSchema,\n415\t    });\n416\t  }\n417\t}\n418\t\n419\t/**\n420\t * Execute an explicit READ against a SOURCE schema for the few reads that are\n421\t * cross-tenant BY DESIGN: seeded reference tables (e.g. `farm.equipment_types`)\n422\t * and gateway-enforced federation `__resolveReference` lookups that arrive with\n423\t * no tenant context.\n424\t *\n425\t * This is the SANCTIONED counterpart to `runInTenantRead`. It opens a READ ONLY\n426\t * transaction, pins `search_path` to the SOURCE schema only (no tenant schema),\n427\t * sets `app.bypass_rls = 'on'` transaction-locally (the tenant-isolation policy\n428\t * must not deny a deliberately cross-tenant read), and asserts `current_schema()`\n429\t * actually resolved to the source schema before any domain query runs.\n430\t *\n431\t * Callers MUST be on an explicitly cross-tenant path — tenant-owned reads use\n432\t * `runInTenantRead`. Routing a cross-tenant read here (instead of an ad-hoc raw\n433\t * `dataSource.query` with a hand-written `\"schema\".\"table\"`) keeps every\n434\t * source-schema read auditable in one place.\n435\t */\n436\texport async function runInSourceRead<T>(\n437\t  dataSource: DataSource,\n438\t  sourceSchema: string,\n439\t  fn: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n440\t): Promise<T> {\n441\t  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n442\t    throw new Error(`runInSourceRead: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n443\t  }\n444\t\n445\t  const startedAt = Date.now();\n446\t  const queryRunner = dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n447\t  await queryRunner.connect();\n448\t  await queryRunner.startTransaction('READ COMMITTED');\n449\t\n450\t  try {\n451\t    await queryRunner.query('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY');\n452\t    await queryRunner.query(`SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', $1, true)`, [\n453\t      `\"${sourceSchema}\", public`,\n454\t    ]);\n455\t    // Cross-tenant by design: the tenant-isolation policy USING clause honors\n456\t    // `app.bypass_rls = 'on'`, so a reference / federation read is not denied.\n457\t    await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, 'on', true)`, [RLS_BYPASS_GUC]);\n458\t    await assertSourceReadContext(queryRunner, sourceSchema);\n459\t    const result = await fn(queryRunner);\n460\t    await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n461\t    traceTenantBoundary({\n462\t      operation: 'source-read',\n463\t      sourceSchema,\n464\t      resultState: 'SUCCESS',\n465\t      startedAt,\n466\t      result,\n467\t    });\n468\t    return result;\n469\t  } catch (error) {\n470\t    await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n471\t    traceTenantBoundary({\n472\t      operation: 'source-read',\n473\t      sourceSchema,\n474\t      resultState: resultStateForError(error),\n475\t      startedAt,\n476\t      error,\n477\t    });\n478\t    throw error;\n479\t  } finally {\n480\t { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { DataSource, QueryRunner } from 'typeorm';\n\nimport { withTenantContext } from '../context/with-tenant-context';\nimport { getRequestContext } from '../logging/request-context';\n\nimport { RLS_BYPASS_GUC, RLS_TENANT_GUC } from './rls/apply-tenant-rls.helper';\nimport { validateTenantSchemaName } from './schema-manager.service';\nimport { TenantContextError } from './tenant-context-error';\nimport { getTenantSchemaName, isValidUUID } from './tenant-schema.utils';\n\nconst SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE = /^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$/;\n\n/**\n * Tenant boundary observability (Farm Data SSOT plan §3-F / §8.7).\n *\n * Every boundary execution emits one structured trace so a read's tenant\n * routing is observable end to end. `resultState` is the FarmDataReadTrace\n * taxonomy: a verified `SUCCESS`, or the exact failure mode that previously\n * produced a silent empty result. SUCCESS traces are debug-level (off in prod\n * by default); a tenant-context mismatch is warn-level. Emitting the trace must\n * NEVER break a read — any error inside the tracer is swallowed.\n */\nexport type TenantBoundaryResultState = 'SUCCESS' | 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH' | 'RLS_MISMATCH' | 'ERROR';\n\nconst boundaryLogger = new Logger('TenantBoundary');\n\n/** Best-effort row count for the common array / paginated-`data` result shapes. */\nfunction rowCountOf(result: unknown): number | undefined {\n  if (Array.isArray(result)) return result.length;\n  if (result && typeof result === 'object' && Array.isArray((result as { data?: unknown }).data)) {\n    return (result as { data: unknown[] }).data.length;\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\ninterface TenantBoundaryTraceInput {\n  readonly operation: 'tenant-read' | 'tenant-transaction' | 'source-read';\n  readonly sourceSchema: string;\n  readonly tenantId?: string;\n  readonly resultState: TenantBoundaryResultState;\n  readonly startedAt: number;\n  readonly result?: unknown;\n  readonly error?: unknown;\n}\n\nfunction traceTenantBoundary(input: TenantBoundaryTraceInput): void {\n  try {\n    const ctx = getRequestContext();\n    const trace = {\n      event: 'TenantBoundaryTrace',\n      operation: input.operation,\n      resultState: input.resultState,\n      correlationId: ctx.correlationId,\n      traceId: ctx.traceId,\n      durationMs: Date.now() - input.startedAt,\n      rowCount: rowCountOf(input.result),\n    };\n    if (input.resultState === 'SUCCESS') {\n      // Success is one trace per read (high volume). It is OPT-IN — enable with\n      // FARM_DATA_READ_TRACE=on for deep per-read diagnostics. A tenant-context\n      // mismatch ALWAYS emits (warn) regardless of the flag.\n      if (process.env['FARM_DATA_READ_TRACE'] === 'on') {\n        boundaryLogger.debug(JSON.stringify(trace));\n      }\n    } else {\n      boundaryLogger.warn(JSON.stringify(trace));\n    }\n  } catch {\n    // Observability must never break a read.\n  }\n}\n\nfunction resultStateForError(error: unknown): TenantBoundaryResultState {\n  if (error instanceof TenantContextError) {\n    return error.state === 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH'\n      ? 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH'\n      : error.state === 'RLS_MISMATCH'\n        ? 'RLS_MISMATCH'\n        : 'ERROR';\n  }\n  return 'ERROR';\n}\n\n/**\n * Pin a QueryRunner transaction to one tenant schema.\n *\n * This is the transaction-level counterpart to TenantConnectionBootstrap's\n * pool checkout patch. Checkout routing is necessary but not sufficient for\n * long-lived transaction code: every transactional write should explicitly\n * assert its own `search_path` after `START TRANSACTION`, so a stale pooled\n * session or missing AsyncLocalStorage frame cannot write source-schema data.\n */\nexport async function pinTenantTransactionSearchPath(\n  queryRunner: QueryRunner,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n  tenantId: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`pinTenantTransactionSearchPath: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n  }\n  if (!isValidUUID(tenantId)) {\n    throw new Error(`pinTenantTransactionSearchPath: invalid tenantId \"${tenantId}\"`);\n  }\n\n  const tenantSchema = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n  await pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantSchema);\n}\n\nexport async function pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath(\n  queryRunner: QueryRunner,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n  tenantSchema: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `pinTenantSchemaTransactionSearchPath: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`,\n    );\n  }\n  validateTenantSchemaName(tenantSchema);\n\n  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', $1, true)`, [\n    `\"${tenantSchema}\", \"${sourceSchema}\", public`,\n  ]);\n}\n\n/**\n * Set the `app.current_tenant` RLS GUC transaction-locally and ASSERT that the\n * live connection actually resolves to the expected tenant schema + tenant id\n * BEFORE any domain query runs.\n *\n * This is the load-bearing fail-closed guarantee (Farm Data SSOT plan §5-1).\n * `RlsConnectionBootstrap` sets the GUC on pool checkout, but nothing today\n * verifies it actually took effect on the connection a handler ends up using —\n * so an unset GUC (RLS denies all rows) or a missing tenant schema (search_path\n * silently falls back to the source schema) both produce an empty result that\n * is indistinguishable from a legitimately-empty table. By owning the GUC\n * transaction-locally and reading back `current_schema()` + the GUC, the\n * boundary turns those two silent failure modes into a hard `TenantContextError`.\n *\n * `current_schema()` returns the first EXISTING schema in `search_path`, so a\n * missing/un-provisioned tenant schema (which falls through to the source\n * schema) is caught as a `SCHEMA_MISMATCH`.\n */\n/**\n * The single capability the boundary assertion needs from a connection. A full\n * TypeORM `QueryRunner` satisfies this, but narrowing to it keeps the assertion\n * unit-testable with a minimal mock and no unsafe double cast.\n */\nexport interface TenantContextQueryExecutor {\n  query(sql: string, parameters?: unknown[]): Promise<unknown>;\n}\n\n/**\n * Bind the RLS controls transaction-locally and verify they took.\n *\n * ORPHAN-CRITICAL-573 — extracted from `assertTenantTransactionContext`\n * because auth-service needs exactly this and NOT the schema half of it: its\n * tenant lifecycle writes land in the SOURCE schema (`auth.*`), and during\n * provisioning the tenant schema does not exist yet. Calling the full\n * assertion there would trade one failure for another.\n *\n * What it guarantees: `app.current_tenant` is this tenant, `app.bypass_rls`\n * is off, both transaction-locally so a pooled connection cannot carry either\n * into the next caller — and both READ BACK, so a GUC that silently failed to\n * apply becomes a named `TenantContextError` instead of an RLS refusal whose\n * message names a table and not a cause. That distinction is the whole reason\n * tenant onboarding stayed broken for months.\n */\nexport async function bindTenantRlsContext(\n  queryRunner: TenantContextQueryExecutor,\n  tenantId: string,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`bindTenantRlsContext: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n  }\n  if (!isValidUUID(tenantId)) {\n    throw new Error(`bindTenantRlsContext: invalid tenantId \"${tenantId}\"`);\n  }\n\n  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, $2, true)`, [RLS_TENANT_GUC, tenantId]);\n  // A pooled connection may previously have served an audited system path;\n  // a stale `on` would expose every row the policy is supposed to hide.\n  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, 'off', true)`, [RLS_BYPASS_GUC]);\n\n  const rows = await queryRunner.query(\n    `SELECT current_setting($1, true) AS tenant,\n            current_setting($2, true) AS bypass`,\n    [RLS_TENANT_GUC, RLS_BYPASS_GUC],\n  );\n  const row = (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows) as\n    | { tenant?: string | null; bypass?: string | null }\n    | undefined\n    | null;\n  if (!row) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const resolvedTenant: string | null = row.tenant ?? null;\n  const resolvedBypass: string | null = row.bypass ?? null;\n  if (resolvedTenant !== tenantId || (resolvedBypass ?? 'off') !== 'off') {\n    throw new TenantContextError({\n      state: 'RLS_MISMATCH',\n      expectedSchema: getTenantSchemaName(tenantId),\n      // No schema claim is being made here: this binding is used by writers\n      // whose tables live in the source schema, so `resolvedSchema` stays\n      // null rather than asserting something the caller never asked for.\n      resolvedSchema: null,\n      sourceSchema,\n    });\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function assertTenantTransactionContext(\n  queryRunner: TenantContextQueryExecutor,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n  tenantId: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`assertTenantTransactionContext: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n  }\n  if (!isValidUUID(tenantId)) {\n    throw new Error(`assertTenantTransactionContext: invalid tenantId \"${tenantId}\"`);\n  }\n\n  const expectedSchema = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n\n  // The boundary OWNS the RLS GUC transaction-locally so it cannot be left\n  // unset by a missing checkout patch or inherited stale from a pooled session.\n  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, $2, true)`, [RLS_TENANT_GUC, tenantId]);\n  // A pooled connection may previously have served an audited system path.\n  // Tenant transactions own both RLS controls transaction-locally: setting\n  // the tenant UUID without forcing bypass off would leave a stale `on`\n  // capable of exposing every row in the otherwise-correct tenant schema.\n  await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, 'off', true)`, [RLS_BYPASS_GUC]);\n\n  // Read back the schema + GUC the connection ACTUALLY resolves to.\n  const rows = await queryRunner.query(\n    `SELECT current_schema() AS schema,\n            current_setting($1, true) AS tenant,\n            current_setting($2, true) AS bypass`,\n    [RLS_TENANT_GUC, RLS_BYPASS_GUC],\n  );\n  const row = (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows) as\n    | { schema?: string | null; tenant?: string | null; bypass?: string | null }\n    | undefined\n    | null;\n\n  // A live Postgres connection ALWAYS returns exactly one row for\n  // `SELECT current_schema()`. The only way `row` is absent is a unit-test\n  // mock with no backing connection — there is nothing live to assert against,\n  // so skip. Every real execution returns a row and is therefore verified.\n  if (!row) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const resolvedSchema: string | null = row.schema ?? null;\n  const resolvedTenant: string | null = row.tenant ?? null;\n  const resolvedBypass: string | null = row.bypass ?? null;\n\n  if (resolvedSchema !== expectedSchema) {\n    throw new TenantContextError({\n      state: 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH',\n      expectedSchema,\n      resolvedSchema,\n      sourceSchema,\n    });\n  }\n  if (\n    !resolvedTenant ||\n    resolvedTenant.toLowerCase() !== tenantId.toLowerCase() ||\n    resolvedBypass !== 'off'\n  ) {\n    throw new TenantContextError({\n      state: 'RLS_MISMATCH',\n      expectedSchema,\n      resolvedSchema,\n      sourceSchema,\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Execute a TypeORM QueryRunner transaction inside a fail-closed tenant\n * context and with transaction-local search_path pinning.\n */\nexport async function runInTenantTransaction<T>(\n  dataSource: DataSource,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n  tenantId: string,\n  fn: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n): Promise<T> {\n  return withTenantContext(tenantId, async () => {\n    const startedAt = Date.now();\n    const queryRunner = dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n    await queryRunner.connect();\n    await queryRunner.startTransaction();\n\n    try {\n      await pinTenantTransactionSearchPath(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n      await assertTenantTransactionContext(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n      const result = await fn(queryRunner);\n      await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n      traceTenantBoundary({\n        operation: 'tenant-transaction',\n        sourceSchema,\n        tenantId,\n        resultState: 'SUCCESS',\n        startedAt,\n        result,\n      });\n      return result;\n    } catch (error) {\n      await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n      traceTenantBoundary({\n        operation: 'tenant-transaction',\n        sourceSchema,\n        tenantId,\n        resultState: resultStateForError(error),\n        startedAt,\n        error,\n      });\n      throw error;\n    } finally {\n      await queryRunner.release();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Execute tenant-scoped reads in an explicit read-only transaction.\n *\n * Request middleware pins pooled sessions, but query handlers also need an\n * explicit boundary so non-HTTP execution paths cannot accidentally read the\n * source schema. A read-only transaction gives `set_config(..., true)` a stable\n * transaction scope and makes accidental writes structurally fail.\n */\nexport async function runInTenantRead<T>(\n  dataSource: DataSource,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n  tenantId: string,\n  fn: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n): Promise<T> {\n  return withTenantContext(tenantId, async () => {\n    const startedAt = Date.now();\n    const queryRunner = dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n    await queryRunner.connect();\n    await queryRunner.startTransaction('READ COMMITTED');\n\n    try {\n      await queryRunner.query('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY');\n      await pinTenantTransactionSearchPath(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n      await assertTenantTransactionContext(queryRunner, sourceSchema, tenantId);\n      const result = await fn(queryRunner);\n      await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n      traceTenantBoundary({\n        operation: 'tenant-read',\n        sourceSchema,\n        tenantId,\n        resultState: 'SUCCESS',\n        startedAt,\n        result,\n      });\n      return result;\n    } catch (error) {\n      await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n      traceTenantBoundary({\n        operation: 'tenant-read',\n        sourceSchema,\n        tenantId,\n        resultState: resultStateForError(error),\n        startedAt,\n        error,\n      });\n      throw error;\n    } finally {\n      await queryRunner.release();\n    }\n  });\n}\n\n/**\n * Assert the connection actually resolved to the expected SOURCE schema before a\n * source read runs. A stray tenant schema left in `search_path` (e.g. an\n * inherited pooled session) would otherwise make a \"source\" read silently\n * resolve a tenant's shadow copy.\n */\nexport async function assertSourceReadContext(\n  queryRunner: TenantContextQueryExecutor,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n): Promise<void> {\n  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`assertSourceReadContext: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n  }\n\n  const rows = await queryRunner.query(`SELECT current_schema() AS schema`);\n  const row = (Array.isArray(rows) ? rows[0] : rows) as\n    | { schema?: string | null }\n    | undefined\n    | null;\n\n  // Absent row ⇒ unit-test mock with no backing connection; nothing to assert.\n  if (!row) {\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const resolvedSchema: string | null = row.schema ?? null;\n  if (resolvedSchema !== sourceSchema) {\n    throw new TenantContextError({\n      state: 'SCHEMA_MISMATCH',\n      expectedSchema: sourceSchema,\n      resolvedSchema,\n      sourceSchema,\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Execute an explicit READ against a SOURCE schema for the few reads that are\n * cross-tenant BY DESIGN: seeded reference tables (e.g. `farm.equipment_types`)\n * and gateway-enforced federation `__resolveReference` lookups that arrive with\n * no tenant context.\n *\n * This is the SANCTIONED counterpart to `runInTenantRead`. It opens a READ ONLY\n * transaction, pins `search_path` to the SOURCE schema only (no tenant schema),\n * sets `app.bypass_rls = 'on'` transaction-locally (the tenant-isolation policy\n * must not deny a deliberately cross-tenant read), and asserts `current_schema()`\n * actually resolved to the source schema before any domain query runs.\n *\n * Callers MUST be on an explicitly cross-tenant path — tenant-owned reads use\n * `runInTenantRead`. Routing a cross-tenant read here (instead of an ad-hoc raw\n * `dataSource.query` with a hand-written `\"schema\".\"table\"`) keeps every\n * source-schema read auditable in one place.\n */\nexport async function runInSourceRead<T>(\n  dataSource: DataSource,\n  sourceSchema: string,\n  fn: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n): Promise<T> {\n  if (!SOURCE_SCHEMA_RE.test(sourceSchema)) {\n    throw new Error(`runInSourceRead: invalid source schema \"${sourceSchema}\"`);\n  }\n\n  const startedAt = Date.now();\n  const queryRunner = dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n  await queryRunner.connect();\n  await queryRunner.startTransaction('READ COMMITTED');\n\n  try {\n    await queryRunner.query('SET TRANSACTION READ ONLY');\n    await queryRunner.query(`SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', $1, true)`, [\n      `\"${sourceSchema}\", public`,\n    ]);\n    // Cross-tenant by design: the tenant-isolation policy USING clause honors\n    // `app.bypass_rls = 'on'`, so a reference / federation read is not denied.\n    await queryRunner.query(`SELECT set_config($1, 'on', true)`, [RLS_BYPASS_GUC]);\n    await assertSourceReadContext(queryRunner, sourceSchema);\n    const result = await fn(queryRunner);\n    await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n    traceTenantBoundary({\n      operation: 'source-read',\n      sourceSchema,\n      resultState: 'SUCCESS',\n      startedAt,\n      result,\n    });\n    return result;\n  } catch (error) {\n    await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n    traceTenantBoundary({\n      operation: 'source-read',\n      sourceSchema,\n      resultState: resultStateForError(error),\n      startedAt,\n      error,\n    });\n    throw error;\n  } finally {\n    await queryRunner.release();\n  }\n}\n","numLines":483,"startLine":1,"totalLines":483}}}

content' },\n    MEMBER: { description: 'Regular channel member' },\n  },\n});\n\nregisterEnumType(NotificationPreference, {\n  name: 'NotificationPreference',\n  description: 'Channel notification (result as T[]) : [];\n}\n\nexport function assertDbMigrateDdlAuthority(operation: string): void {\n  if (process.env[DB_MIGRATE_DDL_AUTHORITY_ENV] === '1') {\n    return;\n  }\n  throw new Error(\n    `[db-migrate authority] ${operation} is disabled in runtime services; ` +\n      `run the aqua-db-migrate provisioner instead.`,\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Build the security-critical USING clause for the tenant isolation policy.\n *\n * Exported for unit testing — production code should not call this directly.\n *\n * @internal\n */\nexport function buildTenantPolicyUsingClause(tenantColumn: string): string {\n  assertSafeIdentifier(tenantColumn, 'tenantColumn');\n  return (\n    `(` +\n    `current_setting('${RLS_BYPASS_GUC}', true) = 'on' ` +\n    `OR ` +\n    `\"${tenantColumn}\" = NULLIF(current_setting('${RLS_TENANT_GUC}', true), '')::uuid` +\n    `)`\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Discover every base table in `schema` that has at least one of the\n * candidate `tenantIdColumns`. Returns one entry per table with the FIRST\n * matching column (the order in `tenantIdColumns` is significant — list\n * preferred names first).\n */\nasync function discoverTenantScopedTables(\n  qr: QueryRunner,\n  schema: string,\n  tenantIdColumns: readonly string[],\n  excludeTables: readonly string[],\n  includeTables: readonly string[],\n  logger: RlsHelperLogger,\n): Promise<DiscoveredTable[]> {\n  // Validate every input identifier — the schema name and column names are\n  // interpolated into the SQL below via parameters, but we still validate so\n  // that a misconfigured caller cannot smuggle a payload through the\n  // information_schema query.\n  assertSafeIdentifier(schema, 'schema');\n  for (const col of tenantIdColumns) assertSafeIdentifier(col, 'tenantIdColumn');\n  for (const tbl of excludeTables) assertSafeIdentifier(tbl, 'excludeTable');\n  for (const tbl of includeTables) assertSafeIdentifier(tbl, 'includeTable');\n\n  // information_schema.columns is the portable way to introspect tenant\n  // columns. We filter on table_type = 'BASE TABLE' to skip views and\n  // partitions, and on table_schema = $1 so the helper is schema-scoped (one\n  // helper invocation never touches another schema).\n  const includeFilter =\n    includeTables.length > 0 ? `AND c.table_name = ANY($3::text[])` : '';\n  const params: unknown[] =\n    includeTables.length > 0\n      ? [schema, [...tenantIdColumns], [...includeTables]]\n      : [schema, [...tenantIdColumns]];\n\n  const rows = await queryRows<{\n    table_name: string;\n    column_name: string;\n    udt_name: string;\n  }>(\n    qr,\n    `\n      SELECT c.table_name, c.column_name, c.udt_name\n      FROM information_schema.columns c\n      JOIN information_schema.tables t\n        ON t.table_schema = c.table_schema\n       AND t.table_name   = c.table_name\n       AND t.table_type   = 'BASE TABLE'\n      WHERE c.table_schema = $1\n        AND c.column_name = ANY($2::text[])\n        ${includeFilter}\n      ORDER BY c.table_name,\n               array_position($2::text[], c.column_name)\n    `,\n    params,\n  );\n\n  const callerExcludeSet = new Set(excludeTables);\n  const identityTableSet = new Set<string>(DEFAULT_IDENTITY_TABLES);\n  const discovered = new Map<string, string>();\n\n  // Each table appears once per matching column; the ORDER BY above means the\n  // FIRST row for a given table_name uses the highest-priority column from\n  // `tenantIdColumns`. @Inject(REDIS_CLIENT)\n162\t    private readonly redis: Redis,\n163\t    // MSG-CRITICAL-058: the UserDeleted cascade must delete the MinIO attachment\n164\t    // binaries (the user's PII media) for messages it erases, not just the DB rows.\n165\t    private readonly attachmentObjectPurge: AttachmentObjectPurgeService,\n166\t  ) {}\n167\t\n168\t  /**\n169\t   * SEC-M17: Pin the PostgreSQL search_path to the tenant schema for a transaction.\n170\t   *\n171\t   * Must be called before any tenant-scoped DB operation in NATS handlers,\n172\t   * because there is no HTTP middleware to set the schema automatically.\n173\t   * Validates tenant ID against strict UUID regex before interpolation to\n174\t   * prevent SQL injection via crafted NATS messages from compromised containers.\n175\t   *\n176\t   * @throws Error if tenantId does not match UUID v4 format\n177\t   */\n178\t  private async setTenantSchema(queryRunner: QueryRunner, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {\n179\t    if (!TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(tenantId)) {\n180\t      throw new Error(`SEC-M17: Invalid tenant ID format rejected: ${tenantId.substring(0, 50)}`);\n181\t    }\n182\t    const schemaName = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n183\t    await queryRunner.query(`SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', $1, true)`, [\n184\t      `\"${schemaName}\", \"messaging\", public`,\n185\t    ]);\n186\t  }\n187\t\n188\t  private async withTenantQueryRunner<T>(\n189\t    tenantId: string,\n190\t    work: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n191\t  ): Promise<T> {\n192\t    const queryRunner = this.dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n193\t    await queryRunner.connect();\n194\t    await queryRunner.startTransaction();\n195\t\n196\t    try {\n197\t      await this.setTenantSchema(queryRunner, tenantId);\n198\t      // MSGFIX-FAZ1 (live-diagnosis root cause): search_path ALONE is not\n199\t      // enough — the tenant/messaging schema tables sit under FORCE RLS\n200\t      // with tenant_isolation_policy: a row is visible only when\n201\t      // app.bypass_rls='on' OR \"tenantId\" = app.current_tenant. This path\n202\t      // (unlike the HTTP TenantGuard) never set the GUC, so every NATS\n203\t      // request-reply handler (verifyMembership, getChannelMembers,\n204\t      // getMessageForBroadcast, resolveNotificationRef) silently read 0\n205\t      // rows — WS joins were denied with \"not a member\" and hydration\n206\t      // returned null (evidence: DEPLOY-FAZ1.md §3). bindTenantRlsContext\n207\t      // sets the GUC transaction-locally AND reads it back (also forcing a\n208\t      // stale bypass 'off') — the canonical backend-common pattern.\n209\t      await bindTenantRlsContext(queryRunner, tenantId, 'messaging');\n210\t      const result = await work(queryRunner);\n211\t      await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n212\t      return result;\n213\t    } catch (error) {\n214\t      if (queryRunner.isTransactionActive) {\n215\t        try {\n216\t          await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n217\t        } catch (rollbackError: unknown) {\n218\t          this.logger.error(\n219\t            `Tenant NATS transaction rollback failed:    private readonly mediaService: MediaService,\n    @Inject(REDIS_CLIENT)\n    private readonly redis: Redis,\n    // MSG-CRITICAL-058: the UserDeleted cascade must delete the MinIO attachment\n    // binaries (the user's PII media) for messages it erases, not just the DB rows.\n    private readonly attachmentObjectPurge: AttachmentObjectPurgeService,\n  ) {}\n\n  /**\n   * SEC-M17: Pin the PostgreSQL search_path to the tenant schema for a transaction.\n   *\n   * Must be called before any tenant-scoped DB operation in NATS handlers,\n   * because there is no HTTP middleware to set the schema automatically.\n   * Validates tenant ID against strict UUID regex before interpolation to\n   * prevent SQL injection via crafted NATS messages from compromised containers.\n   *\n   * @throws Error if tenantId does not match UUID v4 format\n   */\n  private async setTenantSchema(queryRunner: QueryRunner, tenantId: string): Promise<void> {\n    if (!TENANT_ID_REGEX.test(tenantId)) {\n      throw new Error(`SEC-M17: Invalid tenant ID format rejected: ${tenantId.substring(0, 50)}`);\n    }\n    const schemaName = getTenantSchemaName(tenantId);\n    await queryRunner.query(`SELECT pg_catalog.set_config('search_path', $1, true)`, [\n      `\"${schemaName}\", \"messaging\", public`,\n    ]);\n  }\n\n  private async withTenantQueryRunner<T>(\n    tenantId: string,\n    work: (queryRunner: QueryRunner) => Promise<T>,\n  ): Promise<T> {\n    const queryRunner = this.dataSource.createQueryRunner();\n    await queryRunner.connect();\n    await queryRunner.startTransaction();\n\n    try {\n      await this.setTenantSchema(queryRunner, tenantId);\n      // MSGFIX-FAZ1 (live-diagnosis root cause): search_path ALONE is not\n      // enough — the tenant/messaging schema tables sit under FORCE RLS\n      // with tenant_isolation_policy: a row is visible only when\n      // app.bypass_rls='on' OR \"tenantId\" = app.current_tenant. This path\n      // (unlike the HTTP TenantGuard) never set the GUC, so every NATS\n      // request-reply handler (verifyMembership, getChannelMembers,\n      // getMessageForBroadcast, resolveNotificationRef) silently read 0\n      // rows — WS joins were denied with \"not a member\" and hydration\n      // returned null (evidence: DEPLOY-FAZ1.md §3). bindTenantRlsContext\n      // sets the GUC transaction-locally AND reads it back (also forcing a\n      // stale bypass 'off') — the canonical backend-common pattern.\n      await bindTenantRlsContext(queryRunner, tenantId, 'messaging');\n      const result = await work(queryRunner);\n      await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n      return result;\n    } catch (error) {\n      if (queryRunner.isTransactionActive) {\n        try {\n          await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n        } catch (rollbackError: unknown) {\n          this.logger.error(\n            `Tenant NATS transaction rollback failed: ${errorMessage(rollbackError)}`,","numLines":60,"startLine":160,"totalLines":764}}}
